# ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.pde.runtime/icons/obj16/package_obj@2x.png) fa
FunctionalAnalysis aims at defining the system engineering usual functional breakdown and functional data flow language (close to the UML Activity machine and SysML Activity as Block, partially).

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *AbstractFunctionalArchitecture*

a base class supporting the definition of architectures stating the functional interactions between entities

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Namespace](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namespace),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SystemAnalysis.gif) [SystemAnalysis](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-systemanalysis), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/EPBSArchitecture.gif) [EPBSArchitecture](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-epbs#-epbsarchitecture), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/LogicalArchitecture.gif) [LogicalArchitecture](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-logicalarchitecture), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/OperationalAnalysis.gif) [OperationalAnalysis](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-operationalanalysis), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalArchitecture.gif) [PhysicalArchitecture](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-pa#-physicalarchitecture)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/FunctionalChain.gif) FunctionalChain

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

A functional chain is a set of Functions, activated through an activation graph (or path) and carrying non functional properties such as latency, criticity level ... 
It provides a high-level description of a contribution of the system, users or external entities to an operational capability.

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/OperationalProcess.gif) [OperationalProcess](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-operationalprocess)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *AbstractFunctionalChainContainer*

Base class for possible containers of functional chains (may be both functional or use case containers)

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SystemFunction.gif) [SystemFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-systemfunction), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Capability.gif) [Capability](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-capability), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/LogicalFunction.gif) [LogicalFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-logicalfunction), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/CapabilityRealization.gif) [CapabilityRealization](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-capabilityrealization), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/OperationalActivity.gif) [OperationalActivity](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-operationalactivity), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/OperationalCapability.gif) [OperationalCapability](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-operationalcapability), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalFunction.gif) [PhysicalFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-pa#-physicalfunction)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *FunctionalChainInvolvement*

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

specifies the involvement of a model element in a functional chain

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/FunctionOutputPort.gif) FunctionOutputPort

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

an output interface of its owning function, to be the origin of functional exchanges towards other functions

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *AbstractFunctionAllocation*

a base class for deriving allocation relationships between a function, and some other model element

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *AbstractFunction*

Specifies an operation or an action that is performed by an entity.

A transformation of inputs to outputs that may include the creation, monitoring, modification or destruction of elements, or a null transformation.
[source: SysML glossary for SysML v1.0]

This is an abstract base class for the derivation of specific function types at each design level

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Namespace](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namespace),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

|ownedFunctions|![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-fa#-abstractfunction)|0..*|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#ownedfunctions "the functions that are owned (in terms of model structure) by this function")  |
|*subFunctions*|![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractFunction](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-fa#-abstractfunction)|0..*|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#subfunctions "(automatically computed) the children functions of this function") [![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.debug.ui/icons/full/obj16/read_obj.png)](#subfunctions "pattern AbstractFunction__subFunctions(self : AbstractFunction, target : AbstractFunction) { 	// sub function directly in function 	AbstractFunction.ownedFunctions(self, target); } or { // sub function in function first level package 	find _AbstractFunction__ownedFunctionPkgs(self, pkg); 	find _FunctionPkg__ownedFunctions(pkg, target); } or { // sub function in function first level package sub packages 	find _AbstractFunction__ownedFunctionPkgs(self, pkg); 	find _FunctionPkg__ownedFunctionPkgs+(pkg, subpkg); 	find _FunctionPkg__ownedFunctions(subpkg, target); }  private pattern _AbstractFunction__ownedFunctionPkgs(af : AbstractFunction, ownedpkg : FunctionPkg) { 	OperationalActivity.ownedOperationalActivityPkgs(af, ownedpkg); } or { 	SystemFunction.ownedSystemFunctionPkgs(af, ownedpkg); } or { 	LogicalFunction.ownedLogicalFunctionPkgs(af, ownedpkg); } or { 	PhysicalFunction.ownedPhysicalFunctionPkgs(af, ownedpkg); }  private pattern _FunctionPkg__ownedFunctionPkgs(pkg : FunctionPkg, ownedpkg : FunctionPkg) { 	OperationalActivityPkg.ownedOperationalActivityPkgs(pkg, ownedpkg); } or { 	SystemFunctionPkg.ownedSystemFunctionPkgs(pkg, ownedpkg); } or { 	LogicalFunctionPkg.ownedLogicalFunctionPkgs(pkg, ownedpkg); } or { 	PhysicalFunctionPkg.ownedPhysicalFunctionPkgs(pkg, ownedpkg); }  private pattern _FunctionPkg__ownedFunctions(pkg : FunctionPkg, af : AbstractFunction) { 	OperationalActivityPkg.ownedOperationalActivities(pkg, af); } or { 	SystemFunctionPkg.ownedSystemFunctions(pkg, af); } or { 	LogicalFunctionPkg.ownedLogicalFunctions(pkg, af); } or { 	PhysicalFunctionPkg.ownedPhysicalFunctions(pkg, af); } ") |

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *FunctionPort*

A port is an interaction point between a block or part and its environment that is connected with other ports via connectors
[source: SysML specification v1.1]

Base abstract class for actual port implementations

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) ComponentExchangeKind

ConnectorKind is an enumeration of the following literal values:
- assembly
Indicates that the connector is an assembly connector.
- delegation
Indicates that the connector is a delegation connector.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|
|--|--|
|ASSEMBLY|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#assembly "Indicates that the connector is an assembly connector.[source: UML superstructure v2.2]")
|DELEGATION|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#delegation "Indicates that the connector is a delegation connector.[source: UML superstructure v2.2]")
|FLOW|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#flow "Describes a flow communication")
|UNSET|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#unset "Communication kind is not set")

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) ComponentPortKind

ComponentPortKind is an enumeration of the following literal values:
standard:
A port is an interaction point between a Block or sub-Block and its environment that supports Exchanges with other ports.
flow:
A FlowPorts is an interaction point through which input and/or output of items such as data, material, or energy may flow

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|
|--|--|
|FLOW|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#flow "Describes a flow port : A FlowPorts is an interaction point through which input and/or output of items such as data, material, or energy may flow[source: SysML specification v1.1]")
|STANDARD|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#standard "Describes a standard port : A port is an interaction point between a Block or sub-Block and its environment that supports Exchanges with other ports.[source: SysML glossary for SysML v1.0]")



---
## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EEnum.gif) OrientationPortKind

ComponentPortKind is an enumeration of the following literal values:
standard:
A port is an interaction point between a Block or sub-Block and its environment that supports Exchanges with other ports.
flow:
A FlowPorts is an interaction point through which input and/or output of items such as data, material, or energy may flow

Common Types: 


|Attribute|Description|
|--|--|
|IN|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#in "used when the port represents an input of the component it is used in")
|INOUT|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#inout "used when the port represents both an input and on output of the component it is used in")
|OUT|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#out "used when the port represents an output of the component it is used in")
|UNSET|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#unset "used when the port orientation is undefined")

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ComponentExchange.gif) ComponentExchange

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

a specialized version of an exchange specification, dedicated to characterize exchanges between components

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ComponentExchangeAllocation.gif) ComponentExchangeAllocation

Mediator class implementing an allocation relationship, between a component exchange, and the element that allocates it

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ComponentExchangeEnd.gif) ComponentExchangeEnd

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

an endpoint of a connection link

A connector end is an endpoint of a connector, which attaches the connector to a connectable element. Each connector
end is part of one connector.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ComponentPort.gif) ComponentPort

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

A component port is the unification of the standard port and the flow port.

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)
